# Observation Ledger

Generated by `tools/build_observation_ledger.py` from `tracking/observation_ledger.json`; edit the JSON, then regenerate. The JSON carries schema `oph.observation_ledger.v5`; the file name keeps the historical V3 label of the lane set its sections group rows by.

The same JSON holds two further sections, rendered at the end of this page: `instruments`, the [Emergent-Instrument Register](#emergent-instrument-register), validated by `tools/build_instrument_register.py`, and `constants`, the [Constants Ancestry](#constants-ancestry), validated by `tools/build_constants_ancestry.py`. Both validators check their rows against the observation rows of the same file.

One row per observation the architecture must reproduce. The closed historical issue #726 is the bootstrap; this committed ledger is the maintained surface. Registered PR-* ids name rows of the premise register (established under the historical issue #727); each row lists the register rows its current status consumes. The row set, rungs, and lane assignments were fixed when the ledger was established. The lane column names the open issue that owns a row's remaining work, beside the historical V3 lane the row was composed under. The ledger records adequacy status. It is not itself evidence: a promotion requires committed receipts.

## Adequacy rungs

- **Formal precursor**: an exact mathematical representation or helper exists, but no theorem attaches it to the observer architecture or a physical readout. It cannot by itself satisfy a physics target.
- **Structural**: the architecture provably carries the law; an exact theorem from the executable specification plus named register rows, machine-checked where the statement permits.
- **Emergent**: simulated observers' records exhibit the law; a preregistered, target-clean instrument run at laptop scale with pinned receipts.
- **Predictive**: a pre-comparison frozen instrument owned by the relevant physics lane binds the row to future data with registered kill bands.

A structural claim whose premises are unregistered is invalid, and an emergent claim without a preregistered instrument is invalid. A predictive row is invalid when it carries status attained while the freeze register holds any target of its fixed row-level contract at a status other than frozen or locked, and this builder rejects such a row. A predictive row whose contract targets carry any other register status takes partial or owed.

An attained structural rung certifies the theorem under its registered hypotheses; it does not assert an empirical identification of every object in that theorem. The [native model audit](../../model/PHYSICAL_AUDIT.md) distinguishes finite theorem, executed observer process and physical correspondence. Local signal, action and clock results need no federation; extension to larger domains needs its own compatibility and error control. The [public-data audit](../research/PUBLIC_DATA_PREDICTION_AUDIT.md) records conditional comparisons with the observed Universe, including the retrospective ACT scalar-tilt result. It changes no adequacy rung, physical instrument verdict or frozen prediction status.

## Status labels

- **attained**: the rung's contract is met inside the stated boundary, with committed receipts.
- **partial**: committed receipts cover part of the row's contract; the boundary column states what is owed.
- **owed**: no qualifying receipt is committed; the live owner of the row's historical lane carries the obligation.

Statuses are conservative: a conditional result is conditional, a declared premise is declared, and postdictions are never predictions. The related freeze targets column names each FZ target of a predictive row with its current proof custody and its registration-time freeze-register status; an attained predictive row requires every target of its fixed row-level contract to carry a frozen or locked register status.

## Emergent-Instrument Register

Rendered by `tools/build_instrument_register.py` from the `instruments` section of `tracking/observation_ledger.json`; edit that section, then regenerate this page with `tools/build_observation_ledger.py`.

One row per simulation-instrument design or frozen instrument of the registered adequacy program. The instruments were registered under the historical lane #737, which is closed; the remaining instrument question belongs to [#1136](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/issues/1136). Each instrument binds to exactly one row of the observation ledger above. SPECIFIED is mutable design work, not a preregistration or verdict. Only a completed decisive instrument explicitly selected by the ledger-control lineage can apply its declared consequence to the bound ledger row.

### Separation from the frozen-prediction ladder

The frozen-prediction ladder (`docs/FROZEN_PREDICTION_LADDER.md`) is reserved for physical predictions against external data. Simulation instruments live on this register and never enter that ladder: an instrument verdict concerns what simulated observers' records exhibit inside the architecture and makes no physical prediction.

Freeze artifacts pin `{path, sha256}` pairs. Paths under `oph-physics-sim/` require a canonical repository URL and full commit pin. When the sibling simulator checkout is present, the validator resolves that exact commit and hashes the Git object bytes for every listed artifact. Every other relative path must exist in this repository and match its pinned digest.

### Policy

This register distinguishes an open SPECIFIED design from a preregistered FROZEN instrument: SPECIFIED means mutable design only, with no freeze, authorization, or evidential verdict. A completed positive verdict can support an observation-ledger rung only when the committed receipts satisfy the frozen decision rule and the ledger-control lineage explicitly selects it. Each ledger control declares the exact row-status consequence of a selected REPLICATED or FAILED verdict and the open premises that verdict cannot remove; a later design or incomplete run cannot overwrite either boundary. A ledger row with no decisive completed instrument carries an explicit null controller and cannot be marked attained; a later decisive verdict must be selected explicitly in the same scientific update that applies its declared ledger consequence. The register is separate from the frozen-prediction ladder, which is reserved for physical predictions against external data. Negative and control outcomes are reported with the same prominence as positive ones. Fail closed on post-hoc analyses labeled as validation, parameters revised after data, silent reruns, omitted controls, and any run whose configuration is not reproducible from committed artifacts. The authenticated Whitney Maxwell software readback is a mathematical execution certificate with prescribed geometric evolution, not a frozen physical-adequacy instrument. Its three instrumented slices and separate 64-slab numerical continuation, the free-sector quantum construction and the analytic charged-matter action do not select or change any instrument verdict or observation-ledger controller. The separate 81-sample charged-scalar trajectory checks the full 68 equations and 13 Gauss constraints on the supplied cone but contains no authenticated observer events or quantum-state history. Its numerical convergence checks, the analytic spatial action consistency, complete interacting metric, essential self-adjointness and explicitly normalized neutral initial-state packet with exact scalar moments are mathematical evidence; they do not select a frozen physical instrument, alter any verdict, or promote an observation-ledger controller. The coupled software observer execution, Jacobi duration readout, invariant real-sector continuum theorem and conservative quantum trial history are mathematical/software evidence only. They do not change a physical instrument verdict, observed postdiction, prospective freeze, calibration import or physical-adequacy rung. The golden-source free-scalar compact-detector certificate and local gauge/Grassmann jet action are mathematical comparisons and exact conditional action checks. They do not instantiate a frozen empirical instrument, alter a controlling verdict or establish a measured world. The separate q=5 authenticated scalar execution and Cartan-scalar current/feedback pilot are conditional mathematical/software evidence. Their exact identities, finite temporal bound and independently replayed numerical readouts do not constitute eligible external measurements, arm a prediction or change any frozen row, instrument verdict or observation classification. The finite native-repair histories, all-schedule scale transfer, seeded refinement covariance, port-transport controls and layered-clock experiments in evidence/observer_dynamics_20260925 are mathematical and software evidence. The schedule ensemble conditions on one load per rung, refinement chains share parents, temporal clock correction consumes the supplied geometry, and the conditional CMB comparison retains supplied primordial and background inputs. These results do not instantiate a frozen physical instrument, alter a controlling verdict or promote a physical adequacy rung. The common classical feedback packet, finite local scalar force preparation, native geometric-source obstruction and action-measure reconstruction are conditional mathematical/software evidence. They keep supplied action, preparation, operation, clock and constitutive assumptions explicit; no quantum outcomes or physical metric/source law are observed. Their independent finite certificates do not instantiate a frozen empirical instrument, select a controlling verdict, arm a prediction or promote any observation-ledger rung. The finite native current recorder, P-dependent binary detector, correctable-record capacity adapter and N4 local-tick certificate run under model/. Their complete-history calculations and seeded receipts support the four OPH-MODEL execution claims under their explicit design contracts. They do not create a prospective freeze, laboratory outcome, instrument verdict or observation-ledger promotion. The native current tape does not implement the separately supplied N4 bare-occupation readout, and the P detector does not discharge the physical Thomson attachment. The scalar-response observer adapter executes a binary field instrument and native feedback under a supplied scalar Hamiltonian, original vacuum and golden refinement family. Its explicit branch states, retained bits and conditional feedback/refinement prediction are software evidence. They create no physical frozen-instrument verdict, eligible external observation or observation-ledger promotion.

### Status enum

- **SPECIFIED**: A design specification is recorded and no freeze has happened. The row carries no freeze artifacts, no freeze time, and no verdict receipts; the decision rule may be a pointer into the specification.
- **FROZEN**: The decision rule, seeds policy, and controls are pinned before any run. The row carries the verbatim decision rule, a non-future UTC freeze time, and hashed freeze artifacts; no verdict receipt exists.
- **RUNNING**: The frozen instrument executes under its pinned configuration. The row carries its freeze fields and no verdict receipts.
- **REPLICATED**: The frozen decision rule returned its positive verdict. The row carries freeze artifacts and verdict receipts, but it supports a ledger rung only when the ledger-control lineage explicitly selects it.
- **FAILED**: The frozen decision rule returned its negative verdict. The row carries freeze artifacts and verdict receipts. When controlling, it applies the ledger control's declared negative consequence with equal prominence.
- **INCONCLUSIVE**: The frozen decision rule returned no verdict. The row carries freeze artifacts and verdict receipts, and the bound ledger row is neither promoted nor demoted by this instrument.
- **VOID**: The registration is withdrawn or superseded. A VOID row issues no verdict, and it carries freeze artifacts exactly when it carries a freeze time.

### Coherence rules (validator, fail-closed)

A SPECIFIED row carries no freeze time, no freeze artifacts, and no verdict receipts. A FROZEN or RUNNING row carries a non-future UTC freeze time, at least one hashed freeze artifact, a decision rule naming REPLICATED, FAILED, and INCONCLUSIVE, and no verdict receipts. A REPLICATED, FAILED, or INCONCLUSIVE row additionally carries at least one verdict receipt. A VOID row issues no verdict and carries freeze artifacts exactly when it carries a freeze time. A REPLICATED instrument supports its observation row only when it is explicitly selected by the ledger-control lineage. Each control declares the exact row status required by REPLICATED and FAILED and the open premises that each verdict must preserve. A represented ledger row with no decisive completed instrument has a null controller and cannot be attained. A later decisive verdict must be explicitly selected before its declared consequence applies. An artifact entry whose path resolves inside this repository is hashed against its pinned digest. External entries require a canonical repository and full commit pin; their Git object bytes are verified whenever the sibling custody checkout exists.

### Ledger-control lineages

- OL-A1: controlling instrument `INS-01`. REPLICATED consequence `attain_row` preserving open premises none; FAILED consequence `owe_row` preserving open premises none. INS-01 remains controlling after its FAILED verdict. A later instrument may supersede it only when that instrument is REPLICATED under its frozen decision rule and is then explicitly selected here. Until both conditions hold, OL-A1 remains owed.
- OL-C5: no completed controlling verdict. REPLICATED consequence `support_only` preserving open premises PR-03; FAILED consequence `block_attainment` preserving open premises PR-03. No completed decisive instrument controls OL-C5. INS-03 is SPECIFIED design work only and cannot move the ledger row. A later decisive verdict must be selected explicitly here in the same scientific update that applies its ledger consequence.

#### INS-01 OL-A1 (1,3) signature replication instrument

- Ledger row OL-A1; live owner [#1136](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/issues/1136) (historical lane #737, closed); status `FAILED`.
- Specification: `code/instrument_specs/OL_A1_SIGNATURE_REPLICATION_SPEC.md`.
- Custody repository: `https://github.com/muellerberndt/oph-physics-sim` at commit `42aa96607f40dae94c5a8b65e9dd8e71e5b6434e`.
- Decision rule: Pointer while SPECIFIED: the binding text is the Tier A decision rule in the specification, copied verbatim into this field at freeze time. Tier A summary: arms A1 = 16384/128/96, A2 = 65536/256/96, and C1 = 16384/128/6 with five declared replicates per arm; observables O1 threshold inertia, O2 robust inertia at relative tau 1e-3 with reference (1,2)/(1,2)/(2,1), O3 degeneracy ratio against the reference band 2e-5..4e-5, O4 cone margin and rung ratio (retained 0.5725, declared band 0.35..0.80), O5 structural diagnostics, and O6 split-half inertia concordance; verdict REPLICATED, FAILED, or INCONCLUSIVE per the frozen rule, and FAILED demotes the OL-A1 emergent rung with equal prominence. Eigenvalue caution: the archived (1,3) threshold verdict rides on one eigenvalue at relative magnitude about 3e-5 against a 1e-12 threshold; at a relative threshold of 1e-3 the robust inertia of the retained rungs is (1,2) with one degenerate direction, matching the independently measured (1,2) receipt, and a fresh seed can flip (1,3) to (2,2) with no change in physical content. Every public sentence about this instrument carries that caution.
- Seeds policy: One fresh seed drawn at freeze time and five declared replicate ids per arm. The held-out split is stride-structured per rung (strides 8/35/141), so train/held-out parity structure is recorded and stratified across replicates. N = 5 resolves seed fragility at the 1-in-5 level only, and the report states so. The runtime environment is single and recorded.
- Lineage predecessor: none. This does not change the controlling verdict.
- Reproducibility boundary: The committed campaign retains the manifest, summary, and fifteen per-arm receipt files but no raw feature matrices or fit captures from which an independent implementation could reconstruct every observable. The simulator's validate-only mode rechecks the committed receipt fields and frozen decision rule using the producer implementation; it is not an independent observable recomputation. The pinned campaign commit is present in the sibling custody checkout and is reachable from the configured GitHub remote's main branch; remote reachability closes only the publication-custody check and does not supply the missing raw captures or an independent observable recomputation. Instrument defect found 2026-08-21: the event chart mixes an integer ancestry-depth coordinate with three spectral coordinates whose spreads differ by about three orders of magnitude (depth standard deviation 29.11 against spectral 0.043 to 0.049 on a 2,048-carrier capture), so the quadratic form was dominated by the depth direction and the spectral directions collapsed to an eigenvalue at 1.3e-05 relative magnitude; the reported signature turned on which side of the inertia cut that degenerate direction fell, which is the recorded seed fragility. Repaired in oph-physics-sim commit 017ec2a by standardize_chart and a standardize flag on the fit, which resolves every direction and makes the signature invariant under per-coordinate rescaling, with the flag defaulting to off so committed receipts replay unchanged. The repair does not restore discriminating power: against the C-ANCESTRY null the null reproduces the data inertia in 4 of 5 permutations before and after, and the held-out cone margin is unchanged and negative, because eigenvalue sign counts do not test attribution to ancestry structure. No repaired rerun was performed on that basis.
- Controls:
  - C-SUPPORT: matched support-density control at 16384/128/6; a fired control reads as mean signature degradation plus halved cross edges.
  - C-ANCESTRY: ancestry-permutation null at zero extra runtime; the null destroys (1,3) on at least 4 of 5 replicates.

#### INS-02 OL-A1 factorial carrier-count and support-size follow-up

- Ledger row OL-A1; live owner [#1136](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/issues/1136) (historical lane #737, closed); status `SPECIFIED`.
- Specification: `code/instrument_specs/OL_A1_FACTORIAL_FOLLOWUP_DESIGN.md`.
- Custody repository: none (unfrozen; no artifacts pinned).
- Decision rule: Design-stage pointer only. The mutable design envelope crosses candidate carrier counts 16,384; 65,536; 131,072; and 262,144 with candidate absolute support sizes 48; 96; and 192 at one observer count fixed at the future preregistration, provisionally 256. A later freeze must report threshold stability, continuous spectral margins, carrier-count and absolute-support-size main effects, their interaction, quantitative ancestry-destruction and synthetic-sensitivity gates, sham equivalence, and cone-margin scaling separately through one fixed estimator before applying a predeclared overall REPLICATED, FAILED, or INCONCLUSIVE rule. No component pass may overwrite a failed control or endpoint, and no component failure may erase a separately reported component pass. These values and rules remain mutable until a target-blind freeze and do not authorize execution.
- Seeds policy: No seed has been drawn. Before a future freeze, a target-blind prospective precision and power calculation must determine the replicate count from declared minimum effects, interval widths, multiplicity, and attrition; no fixed count is presumed sufficient. Fresh seeds form paired blocks spanning every retained factorial cell and control, with cell execution order randomized within each block, no seed redraw or optional stopping, and one recorded runtime environment. Runtime-only pilots may measure cost but may not expose signature, margin, or control outcomes.
- Lineage predecessor: INS-01. This does not change the controlling verdict.
- Reproducibility boundary: This is an open SPECIFIED design, not a preregistration or authorization to run. Candidate carrier counts, absolute support sizes, fixed observer count, replicate count, statistics, and quantitative gates remain mutable until a target-blind freeze. INS-01 remains the controlling completed verdict and OL-A1 remains owed; INS-02 cannot supersede it unless a later frozen run returns REPLICATED and the ledger control explicitly selects it.
- Controls:
  - Degree-preserving ancestry rewiring must preserve carrier count, degree sequence, support counts, and one-point record marginals while breaking lineage correlations.
  - Depth-stratified lineage shuffling must preserve the population at every declared depth while breaking cross-depth family identity.
  - A sham identifier relabeling must preserve the complete ancestry graph and leave the analysis invariant.
  - A synthetic ancestry-sensitive statistic of known sign must pass a predeclared quantitative recovery gate through the same estimator used for the main and ancestry-destroyed cells; it has no promotion authority.
- Freeze artifacts: none (unfrozen row).
- Frozen (UTC): none.
- Verdict receipts: none (no run verdict).

#### INS-03 OL-C5 static phase-fixture validator and future source-bound readout design

- Ledger row OL-C5; live owner [#1136](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/issues/1136) (historical lane #737, closed); status `SPECIFIED`.
- Specification: `code/instrument_specs/INS03_SOURCE_BOUND_PHASE_INSTRUMENT_DESIGN.md`.
- Custody repository: none (unfrozen; no artifacts pinned).
- Decision rule: The implemented v1 validator is static-only. A valid committed synthetic fixture returns STATIC_COMMITTED_FIXTURE_CONFORMANT after exact schema, digest, Hermiticity, Kraus-effect, 0 <= E_i <= I, opposite-effect complement, normalization, and expected-frequency checks. Matrix-unit trace differences are diagnostics, not trace-nonincreasing certificates. Every object claiming producer provenance returns PRODUCER_AUTHENTICATION_UNIMPLEMENTED. Neither result maps to REPLICATED or FAILED and neither changes OL-C5. A future authenticated v2 may be frozen target-blind to evaluate source production, preparation, provenance, custody, phase discrimination, PR-64 readback, controls, and PR-03 additivity; only that separate preregistration may define PASS-to-REPLICATED, FAIL-to-FAILED, and missing-producer-to-INCONCLUSIVE mappings. No numeric placeholder is bound here and this mutable design authorizes no execution.
- Seeds policy: No seed has been drawn. Before a future freeze, every threshold, interval construction, multiplicity family, context and effect list, excluded-input set, missing-data rule, and block count binds target-blind. A prospective precision calculation fixes the block count from declared effects, interval widths, multiplicity, and attrition. One fresh master seed then uses a frozen collision-free derivation to paired block and cell seeds, with execution order randomized within blocks, no redraw, no substitution, no optional stopping, and one recorded runtime environment. Runtime-only pilots may measure cost but may not expose preparation coordinates, outcome counts, endpoints, or control results.
- Lineage predecessor: none. This does not create a controlling verdict.
- Reproducibility boundary: This is an unfrozen mutable design with an implemented static conformance checker, not a preregistration or an authenticated simulator instrument. The row pins no producer, custody repository, seed, freeze artifact, run, raw capture, verdict receipt, source preparation, recorded public outcome, operational readback, provenance, or custody. The v1 synthetic fixture is a transcription of committed objects and its static verdict has no scientific promotion authority. OL-C5 remains partial; PR-03, the public-readback remainder of PR-64, and PR-65 remain open, while PR-04 remains a declared consumed effect. At present, no endpoint in this design supplies the operational derivation required to remove PR-03. For any future authenticated-v2 verdict, the registered consequence contract remains: REPLICATED support_only and FAILED block_attainment; neither verdict alone attains or owes the whole ledger row. OL-C5 has no controlling instrument, and INS-03 neither supersedes INS-01 or INS-02 nor authorizes a simulator run. Any evidential freeze or execution requires a separate immutable authenticated-v2 preregistration and explicit authorization.
- Controls:
  - The v1 checker recomputes each Kraus effect, proves exact two-by-two Hermitian positive-semidefinite certificates for E_i and I-E_i, and requires I-E_i to equal the opposite declared outcome effect.
  - The v1 checker recomputes the matrix-unit trace-action table as a diagnostic and does not require its generally nonzero differences to vanish.
  - The static declared-table comparison carries no promotion authority and returns only STATIC_COMMITTED_FIXTURE_CONFORMANT for the committed synthetic fixture.
  - Every producer-provenance claim is rejected as PRODUCER_AUTHENTICATION_UNIMPLEMENTED until a separately frozen v2 binds an authenticated producer, raw artifacts, run configuration, outcomes, and custody.
- Freeze artifacts: none (unfrozen row).
- Frozen (UTC): none.
- Verdict receipts: none (no run verdict).

Totals: 3 instruments. Status: 2 SPECIFIED, 1 FAILED.

## Constants Ancestry

Rendered by `tools/build_constants_ancestry.py` from the `constants` section of `tracking/observation_ledger.json`; edit that section, then regenerate this page with `tools/build_observation_ledger.py`. [Issue #736](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/issues/736) owns this surface.

One row per constants family in the masses-and-constants lane. Each row declares its complete input ancestry against the premise register: P and N are the only proposed fundamental free numerical parameters (register row PR-17). Measured calibrations, fitted anchors, comparison targets, and transport payloads are separate numerical empirical inputs. Every quantity entering a row is classified in place as a register import, derived arithmetic, external mathematics, or a measured comparison target. A row that consumes a measured value carries that consumption in plain sight, through a **`measured_comparison_target`** entry or a flagged empirical-import register row. Declared premises without a register row are marked as proposed and named descriptively. The postdiction ledger and canonical claim registry are the comparison authorities; this surface classifies ancestry, records falsifiers, and promotes nothing.

The ledger status column carries the status of the owning observation-ledger row, read from `tracking/observation_ledger.json` at build time. The two statuses classify different objects: the status column classifies the artifact a row records, while the ledger status records whether a committed receipt qualifies the physical observation, so the two can differ on one pair and each row states its own pairing.

The certified Thomson endpoint enclosure is the alpha_inv_interval field with central alpha_inv_central, compare-only under the recorded guards; the P fixed-point enclosure behind it is certified in code/P_derivation/runtime/p_interval_contraction_certificate_2026-07-14.json.
- Ancestry:

PR-37 through PR-39 are registered, but promotion beyond diagnostic also requires their bridge contracts to be inhabited or discharged and the measured-target ancestry to be removed; registration alone never promotes the row. The certified Thomson-endpoint enclosure is a bound kill surface for the endpoint transport as declared: the current certificate records the CODATA witness outside that enclosure, with the hadronic payload required to reach the witness outside the measured payload interval, and that record bars the endpoint row from any status above diagnostic. ### What the statuses mean

#### conditional_determination

An exact statement certified conditional on the register rows and declared premises named on its row. The conditions are visible on the row, and the row presents no source-derived endpoint.

#### diagnostic

A certified numeric surface recorded for separation or transport against a measured value without a registered same-quantity bridge. The recorded numbers identify nothing and promote nothing.

#### postdiction_comparison

A row whose inputs include measured values as anchors or witnesses. Results on it are postdictions under the recorded decision rules, and enclosures and decision rules never move after comparison.

#### owed

No value, enclosure, or comparison is on record. The row consumes nothing and names the registration that must land first.

Status changes on this surface are recorded register events, never silent edits. The ancestry surface is part of the anti-cheating boundary of the V3 program.
